GPO Autofish Safety Analysis Report

A Non-Technical Breakdown

The Bottom Line

GPO Autofish is a safe game automation tool. It is not a virus, malware, or a Remote Access Trojan (RAT). It is built only to automate in-game actions and does not have the ability to access your files or connect to the internet.

What the Program Is Built to Do

This analysis confirms the program is built with the following standard automation components:

System Interaction

A standard library for low-level system interaction, ensuring correct scaling on different screens.

IMPORT_NAME             11 (ctypes

Screen Capture

A high-speed screen capture library. This is how the bot 'sees' the game to detect visual cues.

IMPORT_NAME             12 (dxcam

Keyboard Input

A component for managing keyboard hotkeys and input.

IMPORT_NAME              4 (keyboard

Image Processing

Works with Screen Capture. Once an image is taken, numpy helps analyze the pixels.

IMPORT_NAME             13 (numpy

Input Simulation

This is the core of the bot. It simulates mouse clicks and key presses to perform in-game actions and listens for your hotkeys.

IMPORT_NAME              5 (pynput

Multitasking

This allows the bot to run its tasks in the background without freezing the main window.

IMPORT_NAME              3 (threading

GUI (Graphical Interface)

The program uses tkinter to create the windows, buttons, and menus you see.

IMPORT_NAME              0 (tkinter

Windows Integration

Used for basic Windows functions, like making the bot's window stay on top of the game.

IMPORT_NAME             15 (win32api

What the Program CANNOT Do (Malware Check)

To be considered malware, a program needs to be able to perform malicious actions. This program is MISSING all of the key components required for such activity.

MISSING: Internet Connection

Allows programs to send and receive data over the internet. Its absence means this program CANNOT 'phone home'.

MISSING: Web Requests

A library for making web requests (e.g., to an API or website). This is NOT present.

MISSING: Web Requests

Another standard library for accessing web pages. This is NOT present.

MISSING: File/Folder Manipulation

Used for copying, moving, or deleting files and folders. This is NOT present.

MISSING: Running Other Programs

Used to start other programs or commands on your computer. This is NOT present.

MISSING: Data Obfuscation

Often used to disguise data being sent over the network. This is NOT present.

Full Technical Data (for Verification)

For complete transparency, the full, raw disassembly logs for both versions are included below. This is the source data used for the analysis. You do not need to understand this; it is included for experts to verify these findings.
